In the critical to negative opinions, it was particularly criticised that the draft did not sufficiently take into account the structure of Austrian stock companies, which generally have a strong core shareholder. It was not surprising for us that, in the review procedure, precisely those groups opposed the draft, whose economic interests are directly affected by the future commitment to a takeover offer to all shareholders. However, these interests face the interests of investors and the capital market. At present, a large shareholder can sell his stock package without having to share the so-called package surcharge. A future commitment to a takeover offer to all other shareholders in the event of the change of control will reduce the revenue to be achieved by the major shareholder. However, since the takeover offer may be 15 percent below the package price, the interests of the core shareholders will continue to be taken into account. Moreover, in order to further increase the acceptance of the Takeover Act, the Government template will provide for the possibility of so-called opting out, which means that listed stock companies should have the possibility to exclude the obligation to draw up a takeover offer by means of a provision in the Statute.

However, since the current financial compensation ends in 2000, it is important to consider very intensively from all sides – federal, state and local authorities – how a new financial compensation also entails structural reorganisations, taking into account federalism and a necessary state reform in the spirit of the principles of subsidiarity. In keeping with these key facts and cornerstones of fundamental policy and understanding, a structural reorganisation of the financial balance should be sought from all sides with great effort in the new millennium. High house! In the case of family counsellors, the partial regular presence of a doctor is currently conditional on federal funding, and it has been shown that the four-hour regular presence per calendar month is not always necessary. However, it must of course be ensured that the use of a doctor as a result of primary education in the advisory services remains secure. At present, this consultation must be carried out free of charge in the family advisory services. However, the offer of advice is also made by those whose economic situation allows them to make a cost contribution. The consultative bodies are now given the opportunity to remove costs for the consultation by clients who are financially able to do so. With this option, the advice offer can also be extended in future without additional costs for the federal government. I do not hide that there are problems and concerns about anonymity in the issue of invoices and that it is feared that the confidential discussion climate could be affected. It will therefore be necessary to lay down precisely how the cost contribution can be calculated on the economic situation and on the above concerns of those seeking advice. Unequal treatment and arbitrary decisions on the level of fees may and will not occur, however, I am at a cost. The fees shall be governed by a regulation, including in agreement with the Ministry of Finance. Finally, ladies and gentlemen, it can be said that the revision of the Family Advisory Act will lead to greater flexibility, self-determination and cost-reliance. At the same time, despite savings of about 4 million schillings, the public will be given advice which will increase in quality and support, because in such a sensitive area, the offer must be in line with the wishes. That is why I would also like to give my approval to this legislative innovation.

ParlaMint-AT_1997-11-06-020-XX-NRSITZ-00094_d7e10048	Mr President. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! The agricultural market Austria has its activities as a market organisation and agro-marketing facility on 1 January. In July 1993 the Commission adopted a proposal for a Council Directive on the approximation of the laws, regulations and administrative provisions of the Member States relating to the approximation of the laws, regulations and administrative provisions It was established as an EU-compliant institution and designed to work as a service-oriented organisation for the Austrian agricultural sector. Although the preparation period is up to accession to the EU on 1 January, the period of accession to the European Union has been reduced. In January 1995, a strong, administrative organisation, which is also in line with EU rules, was able to put on its feet. The AMA handled 20 out of a total of 22 market orders and paid out almost all agricultural funding. Ladies and gentlemen! The AMA is a public law company. Your main tasks are: Implementation of market rules, implementation of support measures, central market and price reporting, quality improvement measures, promotion of agricultural marketing. The social partners are represented in the AMA, the Board of Directors, which guarantees a balance of interests between all the professional groups involved, from farmers to consumers. Following the accession of the EU, which was managed under enormous time pressure, it is now possible to see a strong increase in the efficiency of the process organisation in the third year of membership. However, there is still time pressure in the activities of the AMA, since certain decisions concerning the implementation of new measures are taken, in part from Brussels and also from the Federal Ministry of Agriculture, relatively late before entry into force and the mandate of the AMA can therefore be held for a long time. The largest AMA task, the EU compensation for all agricultural sectors, has dominated the activity of the AMA in recent years and placed high demands on all its employees. Ladies and gentlemen! We've heard it today: In this year alone, 354,000 applications were submitted by the farmers, with around 21 billion Schilling being paid out. There are also numerous activities of the AMA, such as the preparation of import and export licences, intervention measures, quota administrations and much more. Special attention will also be paid to information on economic participation, which is particularly important in the field of external trade and market support. The AMA's efforts have ultimately led to the full approval as a paying agency by the internal revision of the Federal Ministry of Agriculture and the authorities from Brussels. We've heard it today: International audit bodies not only certify an efficient performance, but also a cost-effective administrative activity compared with other EU market organisations. In connection with the checks, I would like to draw your attention to the fact that, as the President said, the AMA is probably the best-controlled organisation in Austria. The AMA Act provides for the activities of a Monitoring Committee to report to the Management Board on the checks carried out by it on a regular basis. In addition, the State Supervisory Board of the Federal Ministry of Agriculture is represented in the Board of Directors and in all expert advisory committees. Furthermore, the financial conduct is reviewed by an auditor. In accordance with EU rules and requirements, AMA has established an internal audit service that serves as a contact point for all external audit services. Both the Austrian Court of Auditors and the European Court of Auditors are constantly examining the implementation of the measures. The accounts and the external revision of the Federal Ministry of Agriculture are de facto active as ongoing monitoring of payments. Ladies and gentlemen! In this context, it is also important to remember the European Agricultural Guidance and Guarantee Fund, which carries out particularly strict controls on the flow of funds from the EU. From the list of control organisations you can see that the AMA is actually the best-tested organisation in Austria. Of course, there are also mistakes, but the Ministry of Agriculture is doing everything to stop or eliminate mistakes. The AMA itself has the task – which is not always very easy – to review the legality of all funding operations. In total, some 45 000 test contacts took place last year in the context of the implementation of EU measures and in various accompanying measures. A similar number can be expected for 1997. Ladies and gentlemen! As regards the 1997 AMA law Novelle, it is to be said that these are essentially adaptations in the field of AMA accounting. From the previous form of accounting according to the Accounting Act, the principles of camera-listics, which is also applied by the federal government, will be adopted in the future. AMA is also authorised to take over contracts, for example from countries that are closely related to the AMA's scope of action, which will certainly involve synergies. In principle, the AMA law novelle is necessary as it meets the current requirements of modern administration.

I would like to take advantage of this opportunity and introduce to you the free family tax division in its main features: We imagine that the whole income of the family – and the term "family" is very broad, it also includes partnerships and single-earners plus children – as well as taxpayers' contributions as well as the corresponding state transfer payments. This amount shall then be divided by a splitting factor, which may be variable. This is the basis for the calculation of wage or income tax. The resulting wage or income tax is, in turn, multiplied by the splitting factor. An approach based on this model would have the effect of leaving each member of the family free of tax. This model also corresponds to the intentions of the Constitutional Court in a wide range of areas. But immediately it was suggested that this model forces the women back to the stove. – That is absolutely incorrect! So it is even the opposite of the case: There is a redistribution from top to bottom. It has also been said that the management of this model is too complicated. – That too is absolutely incorrect! On the contrary: As a result of the already 2.1 million employees' claims carried out and the self-employed's claims, family income can easily be determined. It would be sufficient to check whether one wants to split family or individual taxation in the statement of income or in the declaration of employment. According to SPÖ, this model of the Freeman is socially unfair. On the contrary: The introduction of a negative tax on correspondingly low incomes, in which the splitting factor no longer works, in turn results in a redistribution from top to bottom. ParlaMint-AT_1997-11-06-020-XX-NRSITZ-00094_d7e16355	Mr President. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! The Construction Savings Act, which we are dealing with today, certainly concerns one of the most popular savings forms throughout Austria; a saving form whose purpose is to create and secure housing. This, of course, also includes the possibility of credit. At a meeting with young citizens, I was made aware that such loans cannot be used for the creation of cooperatives. A young citizen has told me in connection with the loan money – and I believe that she is right – that she would like to make the own funds available through such a construction savings loan. This has not been possible due to the current legal situation. This initiative by the young citizen, however, has also recently given rise to the fact that, in fact, the construction savings fund has always seen a West-East incident: In the East it is eagerly saved, in the West it is re-issued by means of loans, because of the legal forms of housing, but also by the law of ownership, the dwellings that are rather located in the West, while in the East there is a lack of the possibility of own resources financing for cooperative dwellings of this type. This initiative by a young citizen brings us today to amend the building savings bank law. The purpose of the amendment is to ensure that construction savings loans are available not only for the purchase or construction of a home or a dwelling, but also for the possibility of financing the necessary own resources by means of building savings loans. The simultaneous increase in the margin for unsecured loans from currently 100 000 S to 300 000 S will increase the possibility of housing renovation and, in my view, achieve a corresponding economic recovery in this area. Anyone who knows the Vienna living room with its many old building apartments knows that with 100 000 s you can only do a very small apartment renovation. I believe that this is a real initiative. The third point: This innovation also improves consumer policy concerns accordingly, with regard to the clarity, the presentation of changes in credit interest rates. That is why, ladies and gentlemen, I believe that we can give this novelle the right to give its assent.

Van der Bellen and only briefly replicate the consideration that Mr Graf has presented here, namely the Luxembourg model. It is clear that the Luxembourg model would be a very interesting and quite disputable one, but it must be said that there is no housing promotion in Luxembourg in the sense of Austrian housing. I invite Mr Graf to discuss this model of Luxembourg seriously, that he should also talk to the country representatives, the country leaders and the national residents, and ask them what would be better for them: the Luxembourg model or the housing promotion, as we have in Austria in the classical sense. Secondly, I would like to make a few comments in that I believe that what we are deciding here today is very good because, and it has not yet been said so clearly, it is especially in the conurbations, in the cities, that money is being spent on construction savings contracts. And my colleague, Mr President, is quite right: We have around 5 million construction savings contracts, but some 3.5 to 4 million of them come from cities and the rest from rural areas. So far, it has been the case that these loans have been used to finance single- or multi-family houses, row-houses, and now – and this is what I consider to be important to this law which we are discussing today – the possibility is opened up that, in particular in the area of rental housing, non-profit housing, repairs, rent apartments in the old-age housing sector, construction savings loans can be used. All other data and numbers have already been mentioned here. I am also very glad that the novelle is going through the stage in this form, and I am pleased that a joint request made in the Building Committee to encourage the larger opposition party will lead to a common solution and to a common adoption. I'm really glad. – Thank you for your attention.
ParlaMint-AT_1997-11-06-020-XX-NRSITZ-00094_d7e16750	President. — The debate is closed. Voluntary speaking time limit: 5 minutes. - Please.
ParlaMint-AT_1997-11-06-020-XX-NRSITZ-00094_d7e16772	Mr President. Federal Minister! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen! The history of building savings was described as a success story. I believe that in a few weeks, probably in December, we will be able to experience a historic event, namely that the one-millionth most residential unit in Austria will be financed by means of these savings. This is a result of years of activity, but it is a remarkable result that must undoubtedly be described as a special success in the entire history of Austria. I have chosen the results of 1997 so far to show how the development in this area has been in the first nine months of this year alone. In the first nine months of 1997, 561,000 new construction savings contracts were concluded. This is a huge number of new contracts. This year alone, the 30 000 housing units were financed through the five existing construction savings institutes. Mr Stumpfully pointed out that construction savings have become the most popular savings form. It is worth noting that construction savings are much more intensive than savings than life insurance, as savings books, as bonds, as investment funds et cetera. That is to say, all those who have said over the years that one could possibly abolish this form of savings are undoubtedly in contrast to the vast majority of the Austrians. If all five parties present a motion for an amendment today and decide on it, I take this as a confession that all five political groups are now really in favour of saving the building without reservation. For me, the history of building savings is a commitment to property, a commitment to the joy of creating one's own home, one's own apartment. It is a very human-friendly solution, and it is quite simply an incentive for people to support saving for a specific goal. If we can further promote this with this novel, I think we have taken a common positive step. The success story of building savings will continue to be a success story in the next millennium.

ParlaMint-AT_1997-11-06-020-XX-NRSITZ-00094_d7e16881	Mr President. Federal Minister! Since most of my previous speakers have sung the Song on the Bausparkasen success story, let me make two critical comments. We have, as it were, submitted the Bausparassengesetz to the Banking Act, but we have not given the building savings banks the opportunity to act like banks. My criticism is that the credit volume is limited to 1.5 million. [[Intrusion of the Abg. Dr. Höchtl.]] – The credit volume! Please be careful, Mr Höchtl, I have already done so properly. – Everyone knows that with 1.5 million and 1.9 million, including own resources, no single-family house can be built. Many of us would have wished that this sum would be increased. [[President Dr. Brauneder shall take over the chairmanship.]] The second criticism concerns the terms of the contract, although I know that, as legislator, we cannot directly influence these terms of the contract, but perhaps one can influence the building savings banks in order to show more flexibility in terms of registration in the basic book. In the majority of cases, the building savings funds consist of the first-ranking registration. More flexibility would be desirable, namely that they also accept a second or third place registration, if this is still justified. The third point that would be still worthy of improvement – but this is different from building savings bank to construction savings bank – is that waiting time until the loan is allocated often requires interim financing. These inter-financings are, of course, reflected in the overall budget. I hope, Mr President, that citizens will come to you and present these proposals to you. Perhaps then it is easier to deal with improvement steps again. Thank you.
